# STATE
> Standalone/planning state. Read before working; the planning writer records starts and state changes here. Bound work-group workers record execution through the work CLI and do not edit this file.
> Current checkout only; not an atomic lock or a cross-worktree coordination store. Content truth: ssot/. Task detail: tasks/. Notation: FORMAT.md.
> Work-group checkpoints may add a CLI-generated work summary linking to spec/work/<group>.json. Read work board for live progress.
> Running sessions add an active row; autopilot adds inbox lines for the owner to confirm and a report under reports/.

- docs: en  # doc language

- 261105 decided CLUB-49 CLUB-3 T701: what the cap and the retirement chance do at the close → a let-go player outside the 200 ablest is never let go (an unrecorded one is generated again by his club, a registered one stays where he is), while a held free agent pushed out by a newcomer retires; the CLUB-3 chance is drawn, from the free agent's own stream, only for let-go players without a recorded 1 April decision, and 41 stops everyone; newcomers age by PLAYER-17's decline alone and `freeSince` is the first season he is free (alt: record a retirement for every registered player left out — measured 1950–1960 on generation-reference: 396 retirements under 30 and 219 more coaching careers, against 6 and 4 | draw the chance again for players who already decided on 1 April). rs01

- 261105 decided STAFF-49 STAFF-16 T703: how a derived club holder ages when nothing is stored until he changes → he is drawn hired at 25–62 in the season his count started (1930 for a club and post never stored, as the manager market counts) and retires at 70 as a manager does, his club's next holders following on in a chain worked out at read time, so no holder is 70 or older and still nothing is stored; his ability is the club's strength ±8 inside 45–85 and, younger than 42, his ceiling 0.9–1.8 a year short of 42 above it (alt: holders who never age, 90-year-olds in a 1990 career | store every retirement at each close as the managers' are). rs01
